        <script nonce="GbV9B84pVQbDO7W9MUZu6w">
            var ytcsi = {
                gt: function(n) {
                    n = (n || "") + "data_";
                    return ytcsi[n] || (ytcsi[n] = {
                        tick: {},
                        info: {},
                        gel: {
                            preLoggedGelInfos: []
                        }
                    })
                },
                now: window.performance && window.performance.timing && window.performance.now && window.performance.timing.navigationStart ? function() {
                    return window.performance.timing.navigationStart + window.performance.now()
                }
                : function() {
                    return (new Date).getTime()
                }
                ,
                tick: function(l, t, n) {
                    var ticks = ytcsi.gt(n).tick;
                    var v = t || ytcsi.now();
                    if (ticks[l]) {
                        ticks["_" + l] = ticks["_" + l] || [ticks[l]];
                        ticks["_" + l].push(v)
                    }
                    ticks[l] = v
                },
                info: function(k, v, n) {
                    ytcsi.gt(n).info[k] = v
                },
                infoGel: function(p, n) {
                    ytcsi.gt(n).gel.preLoggedGelInfos.push(p)
                },
                setStart: function(t, n) {
                    ytcsi.tick("_start", t, n)
                }
            };
            (function(w, d) {
                function isGecko() {
                    if (!w.navigator)
                        return false;
                    try {
                        if (w.navigator.userAgentData && w.navigator.userAgentData.brands && w.navigator.userAgentData.brands.length) {
                            var brands = w.navigator.userAgentData.brands;
                            var i = 0;
                            for (; i < brands.length; i++)
                                if (brands[i] && brands[i].brand === "Firefox")
                                    return true;
                            return false
                        }
                    } catch (e) {
                        setTimeout(function() {
                            throw e;
                        })
                    }
                    if (!w.navigator.userAgent)
                        return false;
                    var ua = w.navigator.userAgent;
                    return ua.indexOf("Gecko") > 0 && ua.toLowerCase().indexOf("webkit") < 0 && ua.indexOf("Edge") < 0 && ua.indexOf("Trident") < 0 && ua.indexOf("MSIE") < 0
                }

        <script nonce="GbV9B84pVQbDO7W9MUZu6w">
            window.yterr = window.yterr || true;
            window.unhandledErrorMessages = {};
            window.onerror = function(msg, url, line, opt_columnNumber, opt_error) {
                var err;
                if (opt_error)
                    err = opt_error;
                else {
                    err = new Error;
                    err.message = msg;
                    err.fileName = url;
                    err.lineNumber = line;
                    if (!isNaN(opt_columnNumber))
                        err["columnNumber"] = opt_columnNumber
                }
                var message = String(err.message);
                if (!err.message || message in window.unhandledErrorMessages)
                    return;
                window.unhandledErrorMessages[message] = true;
                var img = new Image;
                window.emergencyTimeoutImg = img;
                img.onload = img.onerror = function() {
                    delete window.emergencyTimeoutImg
                }
                ;
                var values = {
                    "client.name": ytcfg.get("INNERTUBE_CONTEXT_CLIENT_NAME"),
                    "client.version": ytcfg.get("INNERTUBE_CONTEXT_CLIENT_VERSION"),
                    "msg": message,
                    "type": "UnhandledWindow" + err.name,
                    "file": err.fileName,
                    "line": err.lineNumber,
                    "stack": (err.stack || "").substr(0, 500)
                };
                var parts = [ytcfg.get("EMERGENCY_BASE_URL", "/error_204?t=jserror&level=ERROR")];
                var key;
                for (key in values) {
                    var value = values[key];
                    if (value)
                        parts.push(key + "=" + encodeURIComponent(value))
                }
                img.src = parts.join("&")
            }
            ;
        </script>

        <script>
            "use strict";
            var _innerWidth = 0;
            var _innerHeight = 0;
            var _fontSize = 16;
            var _width = 0
              , _height = 0;
            function handleResize(baseFontSize) {
                if (baseFontSize === void 0) {
                    baseFontSize = 16;
                }
                var innerWidth = window.innerWidth
                  , innerHeight = window.innerHeight;
                var width = Math.max(innerWidth, innerHeight);
                var height = Math.min(innerWidth, innerHeight);
                _width = width;
                _height = height;
                if (innerWidth === _innerWidth && innerHeight === _innerHeight) {
                    return;
                } else {
                    _innerWidth = innerWidth;
                    _innerHeight = innerHeight;
                }
                var fontSize = baseFontSize;
                if (innerHeight >= innerWidth) {
                    var designWidth = 1080;
                    var designHeight = 1920;
                    if (innerWidth / innerHeight > designWidth / designHeight) {
                        fontSize *= innerHeight / designHeight;
                    } else {
                        fontSize *= innerWidth / designWidth;
                    }
                } else {
                    var designWidth = 2560;
                    var designHeight = 1440;
                    if (innerWidth / innerHeight > designWidth / designHeight) {
                        fontSize *= innerHeight / designHeight;
                    } else {
                        fontSize *= innerWidth / designWidth;
                    }
                }
                const html = document.querySelector("html")
                html.style.fontSize = fontSize + "px";

                if (window.navigator.userAgent.includes("HarmonyOS") || window.navigator.userAgent.includes("OpenHarmony") || window.navigator.userAgent.includes("bdhonorbrowser")) {
                    var viewportMeta = document.querySelector("meta[name=viewport]");
                    var radio = window.devicePixelRatio;
                    viewportMeta === null || viewportMeta === void 0 ? void 0 : viewportMeta.setAttribute("content", "width=" + window.outerWidth * radio + "px, initial-scale=" + (1 / radio).toFixed(3) + ", maximum-scale=" + (1 / radio).toFixed(3) + ", user-scalable=0" + ", viewport-fit=cover");
                }

            }
            handleResize();

.91,1.81-1.54h.1l.11-.16a41.45,41.45,0,0,1,5.16-3.34l.69-.38.37-.16.64-.32.42-.16A35,35,0,0,1,408.05,0ZM60,187.42C53,179,40.55,165,33.8,178.18c-10.56,29.16,8,153.05,17.69,162.56,6.53,7,13.33,10.73,22.78,16.73C100,373.13,142,394.32,180.21,411.82c34.31,1.36,83.79,15.51,115,78.26,3.4,7.17-1.44,15.72-9,15-19.22-1.44-48.16,4.31-45.4,50.87v-.05c.08.54,11.56,81.12,94.95,120.17,46,17.72,100.66,17.7,146.58,10.06,15.78-2.5,27.09-6.75,44.35-12.06,70.1-24.43,161.61-55.92,209.88-101.17,10.25-8.6,24.27-16.89,37.18-22.57,22.83-9.82,37.49-8.71,55.65-9.94,4.3-.31,9.13-1,11.15-4.4,3.29-9-34.52-73.35-71.48-93.85l-.37-.21v0c-12-6.8-36.38-11.37-46.52-19-10.62-9.88-21.67-22.46-28.36-29.69-9.29-10.4-16.73-19.7-25.86-29.1C570.73,263,371,293.58,271.72,402c-.53.16-1-.16-1.47-.69-6.32-7.91-15.09-20-22.58-30.21-6.85-7.81-33.67-52.05-41.58-40.95a14,14,0,0,0-1.49,3.56c-4.3,16.14-11.74,29.79-28,15.61C152,327.15,89.94,229.33,60,187.42ZM369.82,397.35c17.68-11.68,50.77,6.86,74,41.54s27.78,72.42,10.37,84.11c-17.69,11.68-50.78-6.85-74-41.53S352.4,409.24,369.82,397.35ZM422.61,59.53C417.14,36.22,411.4,28.41,406,31.12c0,0-.11,0-.11.05a9.94,9.94,0,0,0-1,.59c-.1.1-.26.21-.37.32-2.07,1.75-4,5-5.84,9.61-10.83,24.59-22.68,83.54-23.21,99.31-.9,22.94,59.38,154.12,68.67,151,12.16-4,56.51-5.52,63.57-5.52S501.36,168.83,491,168a4.3,4.3,0,0,0-2.18.53,2.88,2.88,0,0,0-.69.48c-4.78,3.4-10,15.24-15.19,27-.26.64-.58,1.28-.85,1.91-6.74,12.11-13.7,20.82-17.52,8.87C446.82,182.74,430.68,94.05,422.61,59.53Z"></path>

                        <div class="__20-NoticeDetail_contentContainer__irljr">
                            <div class="__20-NoticeDetail_subtitle__Eu01S">
                                <span class="__20-NoticeDetail_type__fRshy">News</span>
                                <span class="__20-NoticeDetail_date__A4MY4">2025-11-01 12:00</span>
                            </div>
                            <h1 class="__20-NoticeDetail_title__cALu9">Arknights: Endfield Beta Test II: DEV Comm//02</h1>
                            <div class="__20-NoticeDetail_divider__Kqd1D"></div>
                            <div class="__20-NoticeDetail_content__wIAEN">
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Dear Endministrators:</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>This is the Arknights: Endfield Team. Since the end of Beta Test, we've reviewed your invaluable feedback and made various improvements and adjustments. We've also introduced plenty of new content in line with our development plan. With Beta Test II around the corner, we'd like to share an overview of the key updates and new additions in various game modules.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <h1>
                                    <strong>1. AIC Factory</strong>
                                </h1>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Added Regional Development System</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    In Beta Test II, we're introducing a Regional Development System that cohesively integrates <strong>regional exploration, AIC Factory production</strong>, and trading. Your efforts at Endfield Industries will now be directly reflected in the environmental changes of Talos-II.
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Regional Stock Bills</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>As Regional Development Level increases, you can earn a new type of cert called Regional Stock Bills through activities such as regional exploration, Outpost Supplies, and delivery jobs.</p>
                                <p>Regional Stock Bills are a core item in the Regional Development System. You can spend them to make operator gear, redeem critical resources, and upgrade Core AIC Areas. In short, they give you extra rewards for exploring and developing across various regions.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Varied Regional Gameplay</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>We've added various interactive gameplay specific to each region. You can enjoy the gameplay on your own or with others for a more varied experience.</p>
                                <p>For example, Stock Redistribution lets you track price curves and earn profits by buying low and selling high. Depot Node features a goods delivery gameplay where you can transfer and accept delivery jobs for a fun, cooperative delivery experience.</p>
                                <p>In the new region, Wuling, you can expect even more region-specific content.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Outpost Management Cycle</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>In Beta Test II, we've refined the outpost gameplay cycle. Now, you can earn Regional Stock Bills from outposts by supplying goods. The bills can be spent to upgrade and expand outposts to boost their ability to generate even more Regional Stock Bills. You can also spend Regional Stock Bills to expand AIC Factory production and supply more goods to outposts, thereby forming a sustainable production-profit cycle.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/8ecce42ecf351862a4094a21ea97bb1c.gif" data-width="800" data-height="450">
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Regional Development Level &amp; Benefits</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>Regional Development Metrics can be earned in various ways to increase Regional Development Level. As the level increases, advanced development options will become available and new functions will unlock across various regions. You will receive generous rewards throughout this process as well.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/eb617f68a387cb2ad20bc3bb327f99c8.jpg" data-width="1920" data-height="1080">
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Added Blueprint System</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>We've added a blueprint system to make AIC Factory planning and construction easier. Acquire and use various blueprints to quickly set up production lines and expedite development across various regions of Talos-II.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>System Blueprints</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>Completing certain simulations rewards System Blueprints for relevant products. These blueprints feature layouts ranging from basic production setups to complex assembly lines. You can use these layouts to easily replicate and build a complete automated production line.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/149a2b999a7be52169cb0fefc1ad6105.gif" data-width="800" data-height="450">
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Creating and Sharing Blueprints</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>In addition to the preset blueprints, you can save your own production line layouts as My Blueprints and share them with friends both in and outside the game. You can also acquire other players' blueprints through blueprint sharing codes. You can choose to reference their layouts for inspiration or simply replicate them. Either way, your production efficiency will improve significantly.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>New Wuling-Specific AIC Factory Elements</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>In Beta Test II, you'll see Wuling use fluid-based AIC technologies and introduce a new concept called Xiranite. We hope this update provides a distinctive AIC Factory experience.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Xiranite Materials &amp; Technologies</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>A special material from Wuling, Xiranite can be produced with the Forge of the Sky, a regional facility of Wuling. Xiranite is used for regional exploration and for making Wuling-exclusive AIC facilities and operator gear.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Auto-Connecting Power Facilities</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>With the breakthrough Xiranite technology, deployed Xiranite Relays and Xiranite Pylons will automatically connect to the Automation-Core and other Xiranite power facilities within range. Manual connections for power transmission are no longer required, making it easier to transmit power.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/596a879cb7c2f97abc9b2ab231f2df3c.gif" data-width="800" data-height="450">
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Depot Bus Optimization</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>In the previous Beta Test, Depot Buses could only be placed at designated positions, which limited how you could plan your Core AIC Areas. In Wuling, you have more flexibility in arranging Depot Buses by using Depot Bus Ports (as starting points) and Depot Bus Sections.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Added Shared Facilities</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    While exploring Talos-II, you may come across Shared Facilities left behind by other players. These include <strong>Memo Beacons</strong> for leaving messages, <strong>Zipline Facilities</strong> for traversing terrain, and Easy Stashes for storing or retrieving items. The footprints of early explorers can give assistance to those who come later.
                                </p>
                                <p>Shared Facilities you placed can be used by your friends as well and may appear on other players' maps. They connect different Endministrators and form a lasting bond between them.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/cdec4c758692cf45e2385b9d88606832.gif" data-width="800" data-height="450">
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Other AIC Factory Experience Optimizations</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>We've made many other improvements based on feedback from the last test. Here are some of the key changes we'd like to share.</p>
                                <p>Rules for Unlocking Simulations</p>
                                <p>
                                    In Beta Test II, the rules for unlocking simulations have been refined. Now, <strong>unlocking a phase of the AIC Factory Plan will automatically unlock all simulations within that phase</strong>. You can freely choose and explore the AIC Factory tech that interests you most.
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Tips for Formula Details Page</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    We've added the Smart Tips feature to the formula details page. Now, <strong>when a production line is in abnormal condition</strong>, <strong>a notification will appear</strong> on the formula details page<strong> along with a recommended solution</strong>.
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/d2ee9ad0a5266aab358b09f92d237d4f.jpg" data-width="1920" data-height="1191">
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Batch Mode</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>We've added the Batch Mode, which lets you Blueprint, Copy, Move, and Stash existing production lines. In this mode, you can rapidly deploy and edit complex production lines to achieve large-scale production.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Production Line Logistics Facilities</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>We've also added two new facilities: Item Control Port and Pipe Control Port. These facilities allow precise control over the types and quantities of items that can be carried onto a transport belt or pipeline. You can now manage your production lines with unprecedented precision.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <h1>
                                    <strong>2. Combat System</strong>
                                </h1>
                                <p>After reviewing your feedback on the combat system, we've refined and polished the combat experience at a deep level. Our goal is to preserve the strategic depth while making combat smoother, improving feel of control, and expanding tactical variety. Here's a look at the major changes for this test:</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Improved Presentation and Experience of Combat</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>We've made a series of refinements to combat presentation and feel of control. We also added several new features.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Actions</strong>: Remade certain operators' attack and skill animations; adjusted action transitions for most operators to achieve smoother, more natural movements. Refined enemy reaction animations when taking damage.
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/015481bef9ea6f90f69e8fc65ee9bf68.gif" data-width="800" data-height="450">
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Special Effects:</strong> Enhanced attack and skill visuals for most operators; redesigned attack and skill visuals for some operators; refined the visual cues for enemy attack wind-ups.
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Camera: </strong>Refined camera movements and panning during combat to deliver more impactful visuals for releasing combo skills.
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Stagger Node Mechanic:</strong> Introduced a new Stagger Node mechanic for certain powerful enemies. When their Stagger reaches specific nodes, they will temporarily stumble, giving you a valuable window to deal major damage or adjust strategy.
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Others:</strong> Improved combat UI and button prompts. Added options for customizing the buttons of combat UI.
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Underlying Mechanics Optimization</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>We've redesigned the underlying mechanics of Physical Status and Arts Reaction.</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Physical Status</strong>: Introduced the Vulnerable stack mechanic (up to 4 stacks). The Lift and Knock Down effects can add extra Vulnerable stacks; the Crush and Breach effects can consume Vulnerable stacks to trigger various powerful effects.
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/a1c33bb905d2a4107ef33c9cf4c1cd53.png" data-width="1920" data-height="1080">
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Arts Reaction:</strong> Introduced the Arts Infliction stack mechanic (up to 4 stacks). Repeatedly applying the same type of Arts Inflictions on the enemy triggers Arts Bursts and adds extra Arts Infliction stacks; if the enemy already has an Arts Infliction and a different Arts Infliction is applied to them, then all Arts Infliction stacks will be consumed to trigger various Arts Reaction effects.
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/aedbf518817721e933bec19364d5b0b2.png" data-width="1920" data-height="1080">
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>In addition, we've overhauled the Operator Class system and redesigned most operators' skills, talents, and other aspects.</p>
                                <p>We hope that you could make clever use of various debuffs to weaken enemies, activate combo effects, and discover your own combat tactics.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Dodge Control Improvements</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    Operators can now perform<strong> two consecutive dodges </strong>within a short window. Dodging no longer interrupts basic attack sequences, allowing for a smoother attack flow. Timing a dodge right before an enemy attack lands triggers Perfect Dodge, which returns some consumed stamina and restores a small amount of Skill Points (SP).
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/3736a042d7635195b2e9c61076fa8147.gif" data-width="800" data-height="450">
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Improved Teammate AI</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>We've optimized team AI and added coordination moves. Teammates now fight smarter, which makes it easier to achieve team synergy.</p>
                                <ul>
                                    <li>Teammate AI has been refined in attack behaviors. We've added new moves and actions so they behave more naturally and consistently.</li>
                                    <li>
                                        Teammates will now <strong>make smarter use of equipped tacticals </strong>and support you based on real-time situation in battle.
                                    </li>
                                    <li>Teammates' combat voice-overs have also been improved to increase immersion.</li>
                                </ul>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>New Assist Features</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Operator Tags</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>We've added Operator Tags that reflect each operator's combat characteristics. These tags help you quickly understand an operator's strengths and combat style at a glance.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Operator Tutorial Stages</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    All 5-star and 6-star operators now have <strong>exclusive tutorial stages</strong> to help you quickly master their combat styles and techniques for using them.
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Game Tools</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>We've added tools that let you view other players' team setups and combat strategies. We hope these tools help you quickly build your own team and tackle challenges with ease.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <h1>
                                    <strong>3. Stages and Maps</strong>
                                </h1>
                                <p>More stage content has been added and map features have been made more practical, all to improve your exploration experience in Talos-II.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>New Regional Ecology &amp; Gameplay</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>We've created various interactive gameplay tailored to each region's ecology. For Valley IV, we've refined the experience of self-contained stages and updated the existing exploration content from the previous test.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/ae48fe3b362eb9ee200b8d2c78378eb5.gif" data-width="800" data-height="450">
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>For Wuling, our design centers around the regional signature of Fluid. We've added more fluid-based stage mechanics. You can now use jet drones to spray to unlock certain mechanisms or clear Active Blight.</p>
                                <p>Additionally, we've added a wider variety of creatures and enemies with enhanced behavior patterns, so the ecosystem of Talos-II feels more realistic and richly layered.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/f3cd36a29e26978cb92026731c4821e2.png" data-width="1920" data-height="1080">
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Improved Environmental Art</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>Based on the storytelling and concept designs, we've added some special scenic landscapes. As missions progress, these landscapes will undergo unexpected changes. We also refined the art of certain landmark structures to enhance their visual appeal and functionality.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Added Exploration Assist Features</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>You can now purchase Detectors and Compasses. When used, these tools reveal hidden storage crates or Aurylenes within a certain range, making exploration easier.</p>
                                <p>We've also added plenty of new voice-overs and actions for operators. Now, they give timely prompts on which way to go and help collect identified drops, so you can focus more on exploration and the adventure.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <h1>
                                    <strong>4. Refinements to Story and Narrative Performances</strong>
                                </h1>
                                <p>We are very aware of the importance of storytelling and its influence on your experience. That's why we've given a thorough polish and upgrade to the game's storytelling.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Refinements to Story</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>Based on your invaluable feedback from the previous test event, we've reviewed both the main story and side stories and made many changes to narrative pacing and character development. For example, in Beta Test II, you will experience a new chapter of the story unfolding in Wuling. Along with these changes, we've also updated or reworked the relevant cutscenes, audio, and sound effect resources.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Refinements to Narrative Performances</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>We're using new camera techniques and adding new character gestures and actions to bring the operators to life through 3D storytelling. Many side missions now have cutscenes and cinematics to enhance emotional impact.</p>
                                <p>We will continue refining the game's narrative performances to deliver a more immersive audiovisual experience.</p>
                                <p>&nbsp;</p>
                                <h1>
                                    <strong>5. Other New Systems and Features</strong>
                                </h1>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Friends &amp; Reception Room Systems</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>We've added various new features to the Friends System. You can now freely display your weapons, collectibles, and operator CVs in Dijiang. You can show off engraved medals and share your collections and achievements with your friends. You can visit your friends' Reception Rooms, explore their room layouts, or provide production assist to their Dijiang cabins.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/b2028a2863cf3d03a9a7410a44bf93ed.jpg" data-width="1920" data-height="1080">
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Camera System</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>The Camera system lets you freely control the camera view, switch between first-person and third-person perspectives, and adjust parameters such as focal length and aperture. It also allows you to apply filters, change team formations, and add stickers. Use the Camera to capture every amazing moment in Talos-II.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Engraved Medals System</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>You can earn engraved medals by completing in-game missions, achieving regional development goals, clearing combat challenges, and taking part in social activities. Acquired medals can be reforged to higher levels or trimmed to adopt new appearances. We hope this helps you record every milestone in Talos-II.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/2f2355b750b72bbb981c1473b953eb96.jpg" data-width="1654" data-height="853">
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Multi-Layered Maps and Tags</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>The test introduces multi-layered maps. On the map screen, you can view and switch between different layers of the current area to better navigate complex terrain. Custom tags are also available now. You can use these tags to mark important locations for easier navigation.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/5783540283719aa40aca87f0b721a739.jpg" data-width="1920" data-height="1080">
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Switching Endministrator's form</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>You can visit the Stasis Core on Dijiang and use the Protocol Singularity there to switch the Endministrator's form.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/32d07311d2569aa775ef3128e6004b02.jpg" data-width="1920" data-height="1080">
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Controller Mode</strong>
                                </p>
                                <p>We've added a controller mode for this test to provide more control options that fit your play style. Controller support is now available on PC and PS. Support for mobile is in development and will not be available in this test. Thank you for your understanding.</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <h1>
                                    <strong>6. Cross-Platform Adaptation and Optimization</strong>
                                </h1>
                                <p>&nbsp;In Beta Test II, we're providing platform-specific controls for mobile, PC, and PlayStation, so players on each platform have controls optimized for their device.</p>
                                <p>&nbsp;This is the first time we adapt and optimize a game for multiple platforms. We've been continuously updating our technologies and workflows to provide a stable and smooth gaming experience. If you have any feedback or suggestions during the test, please let us know through customer support or surveys. We sincerely appreciate your patience and understanding!"</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <h1>
                                    <strong>7. Localized Experience</strong>
                                </h1>
                                <p>To provide a better localized experience for all Endministrators around the globe, we've thoroughly optimized the in-game text.</p>
                                <p>We've systematically reviewed and revised text in all languages, and refined sentence structures and expressions to better match the reading preferences of local players. For some languages, we've also added the pronunciation of important and special terms to help you understand the game's concepts and setting more accurately.</p>
                                <p>We will keep listening to your feedback and working to enhance the game's localization.</p>
                                <p>In addition to what's mentioned in this DEV Comm, we are developing more in-game systems, new characters, and gameplay modes for Beta Test II.</p>
                                <p>We're continuously refining and polishing game content. If you encounter any issues during the test, please don't hesitate to let us know. Your feedback is invaluable in helping us make the game better.</p>
                                <p>Thank you again for your continued attention and support of Arknights: Endfield! We hope to see you again in Talos-II!</p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <strong>Arknights: Endfield Team</strong>
                                </p>
                            </div>
                        </div>

Other New Systems and Features\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/h1\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFriends \u0026amp; Reception Room Systems\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe've added various new features to the Friends System. You can now freely display your weapons, collectibles, and operator CVs in Dijiang. You can show off engraved medals and share your collections and achievements with your friends. You can visit your friends' Reception Rooms, explore their room layouts, or provide production assist to their Dijiang cabins.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/b2028a2863cf3d03a9a7410a44bf93ed.jpg\" data-width=\"1920\" data-height=\"1080\"\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCamera System\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe Camera system lets you freely control the camera view, switch between first-person and third-person perspectives, and adjust parameters such as focal length and aperture. It also allows you to apply filters, change team formations, and add stickers. Use the Camera to capture every amazing moment in Talos-II.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eEngraved Medals System\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eYou can earn engraved medals by completing in-game missions, achieving regional development goals, clearing combat challenges, and taking part in social activities. Acquired medals can be reforged to higher levels or trimmed to adopt new appearances. We hope this helps you record every milestone in Talos-II.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/2f2355b750b72bbb981c1473b953eb96.jpg\" data-width=\"1654\" data-height=\"853\"\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMulti-Layered Maps and Tags\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe test introduces multi-layered maps. On the map screen, you can view and switch between different layers of the current area to better navigate complex terrain. Custom tags are also available now. You can use these tags to mark important locations for easier navigation.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/5783540283719aa40aca87f0b721a739.jpg\" data-width=\"1920\" data-height=\"1080\"\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSwitching Endministrator's form\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eYou can visit the Stasis Core on Dijiang and use the Protocol Singularity there to switch the Endministrator's form.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20251031/32d07311d2569aa775ef3128e6004b02.jpg\" data-width=\"1920\" data-height=\"1080\"\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eController Mode\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe've added a controller mode for this test to provide more control options that fit your play style. Controller support is now available on PC and PS. Support for mobile is in development and will not be available in this test. Thank you for your understanding.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003ch1\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e6. Cross-Platform Adaptation and Optimization\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/h1\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u0026nbsp;In Beta Test II, we're providing platform-specific controls for mobile, PC, and PlayStation, so players on each platform have controls optimized for their device.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u0026nbsp;This is the first time we adapt and optimize a game for multiple platforms. We've been continuously updating our technologies and workflows to provide a stable and smooth gaming experience. If you have any feedback or suggestions during the test, please let us know through customer support or surveys. We sincerely appreciate your patience and understanding!\"\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003ch1\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e7. Localized Experience\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/h1\u003e\u003cp\u003eTo provide a better localized experience for all Endministrators around the globe, we've thoroughly optimized the in-game text.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe've systematically reviewed and revised text in all languages, and refined sentence structures and expressions to better match the reading preferences of local players. For some languages, we've also added the pronunciation of important and special terms to help you understand the game's concepts and setting more accurately.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe will keep listening to your feedback and working to enhance the game's localization.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIn addition to what's mentioned in this DEV Comm, we are developing more in-game systems, new characters, and gameplay modes for Beta Test II.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe're continuously refining and polishing game content. If you encounter any issues during the test, please don't hesitate to let us know. Your feedback is invaluable in helping us make the game better.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThank you again for your continued attention and support of Arknights: Endfield! We hope to see you again in Talos-II!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eArknights: Endfield Team\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e"])
        </script>

        <script>
            "use strict";
            var _innerWidth = 0;
            var _innerHeight = 0;
            var _fontSize = 16;
            var _width = 0
              , _height = 0;
            function handleResize(baseFontSize) {
                if (baseFontSize === void 0) {
                    baseFontSize = 16;
                }
                var innerWidth = window.innerWidth
                  , innerHeight = window.innerHeight;
                var width = Math.max(innerWidth, innerHeight);
                var height = Math.min(innerWidth, innerHeight);
                _width = width;
                _height = height;
                if (innerWidth === _innerWidth && innerHeight === _innerHeight) {
                    return;
                } else {
                    _innerWidth = innerWidth;
                    _innerHeight = innerHeight;
                }
                var fontSize = baseFontSize;
                if (innerHeight >= innerWidth) {
                    var designWidth = 1080;
                    var designHeight = 1920;
                    if (innerWidth / innerHeight > designWidth / designHeight) {
                        fontSize *= innerHeight / designHeight;
                    } else {
                        fontSize *= innerWidth / designWidth;
                    }
                } else {
                    var designWidth = 2560;
                    var designHeight = 1440;
                    if (innerWidth / innerHeight > designWidth / designHeight) {
                        fontSize *= innerHeight / designHeight;
                    } else {
                        fontSize *= innerWidth / designWidth;
                    }
                }
                const html = document.querySelector("html")
                html.style.fontSize = fontSize + "px";

                if (window.navigator.userAgent.includes("HarmonyOS") || window.navigator.userAgent.includes("OpenHarmony") || window.navigator.userAgent.includes("bdhonorbrowser")) {
                    var viewportMeta = document.querySelector("meta[name=viewport]");
                    var radio = window.devicePixelRatio;
                    viewportMeta === null || viewportMeta === void 0 ? void 0 : viewportMeta.setAttribute("content", "width=" + window.outerWidth * radio + "px, initial-scale=" + (1 / radio).toFixed(3) + ", maximum-scale=" + (1 / radio).toFixed(3) + ", user-scalable=0" + ", viewport-fit=cover");
                }

            }
            handleResize();
        </script>

                        <div class="__20-NoticeDetail_contentContainer__irljr">
                            <div class="__20-NoticeDetail_subtitle__Eu01S">
                                <span class="__20-NoticeDetail_type__fRshy">News</span>
                                <span class="__20-NoticeDetail_date__A4MY4">2026-11-22 10:30</span>
                            </div>
                            <h1 class="__20-NoticeDetail_title__cALu9">[Meeting You in the World o &#x27;Shrooms] Version DEV Comm</h1>
                            <div class="__20-NoticeDetail_divider__Kqd1D"></div>
                            <div class="__20-NoticeDetail_content__wIAEN">
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">
                                        Dear Endministrator,&nbsp;<br>This is the Arknights: Endfield Team.&nbsp;<br>As shadow fades, all things emerge anew. Arknights: Endfield new version [Meeting You in the World o'Shrooms] will officially launch on Nov. 25, 2026. Through this DEV Comm, we would like to share the major optimizations and adjustments that will be made in this version.
                                    </span>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <h1>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); line-height: 1.15;">
                                        <strong>[New Version Guide] Page Now Updated</strong>
                                    </span>
                                </h1>
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">
                                        To make it easier to see what's new, the [New Version Guide] page has been updated with information on the main content and other details about the [Meeting You in the World o'Shrooms] version. Visit the page and hit [Claim Rewards] to receive the following version preview rewards in-game before the next version update:&nbsp;<strong>[Oroberyl]×1000, [T-Creds]×10000, [Elementary Cognitive Carrier]×8, and [Arms INSP Set]×4.</strong>
                                    </span>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">The link to the page has been sent via in-game mail. You can also find it in in-game notices and on our social media channels.</span>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">Go &amp; View:&nbsp;</span>
                                    <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://endfield.gryphline.com/version_briefing/latest?source_from=official">
                                        <span style="color: rgb(30, 144, 255);">https://endfield.gryphline.com/version_briefing/latest?source_from=official</span>
                                    </a>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20261120/f4987c0f8ada55a7d69ff5f609f54e5c.jpg" data-width="1600" data-height="900">
                                <h1>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); line-height: 1.15;">
                                        <strong>New [Smart Picks] Feature in [Game Tools]</strong>
                                    </span>
                                </h1>
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">A new [Smart Picks] feature will be added to the [Team Picks] and [Build Guide] tools. Based on the operators you own, it recommends teams suited to specific stages, along with relevant team building suggestions.</span>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">You can access this feature in-game via [Protosync] &gt; [Game Tools], or visit the official SKPORT community and open [Team Picks] to view the related content.</span>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20261120/b7ee202711985496371d063a42f6b80c.jpg" data-width="2156" data-height="1213">
                                <h1>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); line-height: 1.15;">
                                        <strong>AIC Factory Gameplay Improvements</strong>
                                    </span>
                                </h1>
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">
                                        <strong>· Improved the Placement of Control Ports:</strong>&nbsp;Improved the placement of Item Control Ports and Pipe Control Ports. They can now be placed without first being connected to a transport belt or pipe. They can connect directly to the belt or pipe ports of a facility and will automatically snap into place in the correct orientation.
                                    </span>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20261120/2ab6c40c6566f2567f16699f007ca910.gif" data-width="800" data-height="450">
                                <h1>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); line-height: 1.15;">
                                        <strong>Progression Experience Improvements</strong>
                                    </span>
                                </h1>
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">
                                        <strong>· Added [Wuling Multi-Line Engraving Kit]:&nbsp;</strong>Purchase [Wuling Multi-Line Engraving Kit]×1 with [Wuling Stock Bills] after upgrading [Wuling - Yinglung Pass] Stock Redistribution Terminal to level 4.
                                    </span>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">
                                        <strong>· Improved the Display of Progression Materials:</strong>&nbsp;Improved upgrade prompts on the [Operators - Ability Matrix] screen. When an upgrade requires many types of materials, materials in insufficient quantities are automatically repositioned and displayed first, making it easier to review.
                                    </span>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20261120/aa7f4755378a3724fb0d925b4f4c074a.png" data-width="2048" data-height="1080">
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">
                                        <strong>· Improved Gear Artificing:</strong>&nbsp;Added the new item [Origo-Artificing Fluid Mk Ⅰ] that guarantees success when performing a gear artificing attempt on gold quality Wuling gear. This item can be obtained in the &nbsp;<strong>[Array Building: Mend the Old Gash]</strong>&nbsp;Building Event.
                                    </span>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <h1>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); line-height: 1.15;">
                                        <strong>[Delver of the Cryptic] Special Edition Available</strong>
                                    </span>
                                </h1>
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">
                                        <strong>· Added the new spatial set [Esoteric Mycology]:</strong>&nbsp;[Delver of the Cryptic] will introduce the new spatial set [Esoteric Mycology], which comprises 3 Protocol Spaces. Complete the spatial set puzzle challenges to claim [Oroberyls] and other rewards.
                                    </span>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">This will be the final update for [Delver of the Cryptic] as a permanent game mode. Updates to this mode will pause afterward, but it will return in other forms in the future.</span>
                                </p>
                                <img src="https://web-static.hg-cdn.com/upload/image/20261120/f3cad54f90a65fe427ec4b9296711bef.jpg" data-width="1600" data-height="900">
                                <h1>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); line-height: 1.15;">
                                        <strong>In-development optimizations</strong>
                                    </span>
                                </h1>
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">Here is an early preview of some planned content.</span>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">
                                        <strong>· New Permanent Activity [Main Mission Early Unlock: Gypsosia]</strong>: After the next version update, the new permanent activity [Main Mission Early Unlock: Gypsosia] will be added to the game and accessible via the [Events] screen.
                                    </span>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">After completing the main mission [Chapter Ⅰ Process Ⅰ: Break the Siege], you can use the [Events] screen to unlock the [The Cretaceous Frontiers (Gypsosia)] region and its main missions early. The content of New Horizons Tribute and Beginner's Chartered Tribute will also be adjusted accordingly. You can earn their rewards by completing the specified main missions.</span>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">Main missions of different chapters can coexist. You can return to [Valley IV] or [Wuling] whenever you wish to pick up their stories. You won't miss out on any content from previous chapters.</span>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">
                                        <strong>· Improved Team System:</strong>&nbsp;The maximum number of teams will be increased to 15, so you can preset more teams for various combat situations. A [Team Details] button will also be added to this screen. Click it to view all your teams, then choose a team and click [Deploy] to deploy it.
                                    </span>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">Thank you for your continued interest and support for Arknights: Endfield, Endministrator. In addition to the content introduced in this DEV Comm, additional gameplay features and optimizations are in development and will be introduced in future versions. We look forward to sharing more with you the next time we meet.</span>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <br>
                                </p>
                                <p>
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">We're nearing the end of 2026, and this year's final version update is just around the corner. Our journey in Wuling is about to wrap up, too. Until then, let's traverse the "World o'Shrooms" and meet again at year-end. Our exploration of Talos-II will never cease. New stories and uncharted lands lie ahead, waiting for us to discover them together.</span>
                                </p>
                                <p style="text-align: right;">
                                    <span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">
                                        <strong>Arknights: Endfield Team</strong>
                                    </span>

            "use strict";
            var _innerWidth = 0;
            var _innerHeight = 0;
            var _fontSize = 16;
            var _width = 0
              , _height = 0;
            function handleResize(baseFontSize) {
                if (baseFontSize === void 0) {
                    baseFontSize = 16;
                }
                var innerWidth = window.innerWidth
                  , innerHeight = window.innerHeight;
                var width = Math.max(innerWidth, innerHeight);
                var height = Math.min(innerWidth, innerHeight);
                _width = width;
                _height = height;
                if (innerWidth === _innerWidth && innerHeight === _innerHeight) {
                    return;
                } else {
                    _innerWidth = innerWidth;
                    _innerHeight = innerHeight;
                }
                var fontSize = baseFontSize;
                if (innerHeight >= innerWidth) {
                    var designWidth = 1080;
                    var designHeight = 1920;
                    if (innerWidth / innerHeight > designWidth / designHeight) {
                        fontSize *= innerHeight / designHeight;
                    } else {
                        fontSize *= innerWidth / designWidth;
                    }
                } else {
                    var designWidth = 2560;
                    var designHeight = 1440;
                    if (innerWidth / innerHeight > designWidth / designHeight) {
                        fontSize *= innerHeight / designHeight;
                    } else {
                        fontSize *= innerWidth / designWidth;
                    }
                }
                const html = document.querySelector("html")
                html.style.fontSize = fontSize + "px";

                if (window.navigator.userAgent.includes("HarmonyOS") || window.navigator.userAgent.includes("OpenHarmony") || window.navigator.userAgent.includes("bdhonorbrowser")) {
                    var viewportMeta = document.querySelector("meta[name=viewport]");
                    var radio = window.devicePixelRatio;
                    viewportMeta === null || viewportMeta === void 0 ? void 0 : viewportMeta.setAttribute("content", "width=" + window.outerWidth * radio + "px, initial-scale=" + (1 / radio).toFixed(3) + ", maximum-scale=" + (1 / radio).toFixed(3) + ", user-scalable=0" + ", viewport-fit=cover");
                }

            }
            handleResize();
